## embed (136 tests, 12 files)

### test_embed_bytecode.cpp

- bytecode with trailing garbage never crashes the loader
- bytecode single byte corruption never crashes the loader
- bytecode with hostile 32-bit counts is rejected without huge allocations
- bytecode random garbage is rejected
- bytecode with a valid header but random body never crashes

- embed handles unusual but valid source text

- string exports survive a bundle round trip and run on several threads
- the original module is untouched by string calls
